Make it friendlier.\n   610\t            #          1        2       213    34                45   5\n   611\t            $line =~ s{(\\S+/bin/(quadlet))(\\s.*)(\\s+with\\s+Q\\S+?=)(\\S+)}\n   612\t                      {<span title=\"$1\"><b>$2$3</b></span><span class=\"boring\">$4</span><b>$5</b>};\n   613\t\n   614\t            $current_output = '';\n   615\t        }\n   616\t        # Windows\n   617\t        #                 1   12                        2  3  3\n   618\t        elsif ($line =~ /^(\\s*)(C:\\\\Users.*\\\\podman\\.exe)\\s(.*)$/) {\n   619\t            $line = qq{$1C&gt; <span title=\"$2\"><b>podman.exe</b></span> <b>$3</b>};\n   620\t        }\n   621\t        elsif ($line =~ /^\\s*Error:/ || $line =~ / level=(warning|error) /) {\n   622\t            $line = \"<span class='log-warning'>\" . $line . \"</span>\";\n   623\t        }\n   624\t        elsif ($line =~ /^panic:/) {\n   625\t            $line = \"<span class='log-error'>\" . $line . \"</span>\";\n   626\t        }\n   627\t        else {\n   628\t            $current_output .= ' ' . $line;\n   629\t        }\n   630\t\n   631\t        # One line after each divider, there's a status/timing line.\n   632\t        if ($after_divider == 1) {\n   633\t            # When run with -p, ginkgo emits timing information on the\n   634\t            # first line after the divider. (Without -p, it's at the end).\n   635\t            # Recognize this, because it affects our recognition (below)\n   636\t            # of the test name.\n   637\t            $line =~ s{(\\[(\\d+)\\.\\d+\\s+seconds\\])}{\n   638\t                if ($2 > 5) { \"<b><span class='log-slow'>$1</span></b>\" }\n   639\t                else        { \"<b>$1</b>\" }\n   640\t            }e && --$after_divider;\n   641\t\n   642\t            # Make FAILED and SKIPPING visible. (Only works with ginkgo -p;\n   643\t            # without -p, status is at the bottom of the test block)\n   644\t            if ($line =~ s!^(.*\\[(SKIPPED|FAILED|FLAKEY).*\\].*)!<span class=\"log-\\L$2\\E\">$1</span>!) {\n   645\t                $subtest_status = lc($2);\n   646\t            }\n   647\t\n   648\t            # FIXME: gray out entire block if it's skipped?\n   649\t        }\n   650\t\n   651\t        # Test name recognition, linking, and highlighting.\n   652\t        # The lines after each divider, until the first empty line, are\n   653\t        # one or more test name specifiers, a nested level of Description()\n   654\t        # and It() names:\n   655\t        #    -----------\n   656\t        #    * blah [N seconds]     <<<---- only when run with -p\n   657\t        #    Podman foo\n   658\t        #    /var/tmp/go/src/path/to/this/file.go:lineno\n   659\t        #       podman bar\n   660\t        #       /var/tmp/go/src/path/to/this/file.go:lineno\n   661\t        #\n   662\t        # There may even be three nested levels (e.g., quadlet). We\n   663\t        # look for non-path lines and assemble them -- \"Podman foo\",\n   664\t        # \" Podman foo podman bar\" -- giving each an HTML anchor\n   665\t        # to which we can link from the summary section at bottom.\n   666\t        if ($after_divider <= 10) {      # Assume no more than ~5 levels\n   667\t            if ($line =~ /^\\s*$/) {\n   668\t                # Stop looking when we get to the blank line\n   669\t                $after_divider = 999;\n   670\t            }\n   671\t            elsif ($line =~ /span.*class=.boring./) {\n   672\t                # Sigh. Bindings tests run without -p, and have no blank line\n   673\t                # separator. Use the first command to signal real logs.\n   674\t                # FIXME: can we solve this with ReportBeforeEach()?\n   675\t                #   https://onsi.github.io/ginkgo/#generating-reports-programmatically\n   676\t                $after_divider = 999;\n   677\t            }\n   678\t            elsif ($line =~ m!^\\s*(/\\S+|C:/)!) {\n   679\t                # Source code path: skip\n   680\t            }\n   681\t            elsif ($line =~ /<b/) {\n   682\t                # Already-formatted line (like the \"N seconds\" one): skip\n   683\t            }\n   684\t            elsif ($line =~ /^\\s*(\\[It\\]\\s*)?(.*)/) {\n   685\t                # Test description or name! Remove the \"It\", and make an anchor\n   686\t                $subtest_name .= \" \" if $subtest_name;\n   687\t                $subtest_name .= $2;\n   688\t                my $id = make_id($subtest_name, 'anchor');\n   689\t                $line = \"<a name='t--$id' /><h2 class=\\\"log-$subtest_status\\\">$line</h2>\";\n   690\t\n   691\t                # Special case for tests that fail outside of a Describe()\n   692\t                # block, e.g., global After/BeforeEach. We still want to\n   693\t                # highlight those in the <h2>, but do not include \"TOP-LEVEL\"\n   694\t                # in the anchor name for the actual test name (which will\n   695\t                # come later, a few lines down).\n   696\t                $subtest_name = '' if $subtest_name =~ /^\\s*TOP-LEVEL/;\n   697\t            }\n   698\t        }\n   699\t        ++$after_divider;\n   700\t\n   701\t        # Highlight test name when it appears in the middle of commands.\n   702\t        # But make it boring, because we already have the test name in large\n   703\t        # bold just above. (Except in skipped tests).\n   704\t        $line =~ s!^(\\s*)(\\[It\\]\\s+.*)!$1<span class=\"testname\">$2</span>!;\n   705\t\n   706\t        # Failure name corresponds to a previously-seen block.\n   707\t        #              1     2           2   3  3   4                       4   15           5\n   708\t        if ($line =~ /^(\\s*\\[(FAIL|PANIC!)\\] (.*) \\[(It|BeforeEach|AfterEach)\\] )([A-Za-z-].*)/) {\n   709\t            my ($lhs, $type, $desc, $ginkgo_fluff, $testname) = ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5);\n   710\t            $desc =~ s/^TOP-LEVEL\\s*//;\n   711\t            my $id = make_id(\"$desc $testname\", 'link');\n   712\t\n   713\t            $line = \"<span class=\\\"log-error\\\">$lhs<a href='#t--$id'>$testname</a></span>\";\n   714\t        }\n   715\t\n   716\t        print { $out_fh } \"<span class=\\\"timestamp\\\">$timestamp</span>\"\n   717\t            if $timestamp;\n   718\t        print { $out_fh } $line, \"\\n\";\n   719\t    }\n   720\t\n   721\t    my $have_formatted_log;     # Set on success\n   722\t\n   723\t    if ($out_fh) {\n   724\t        # Summary line for BATS tests\n   725\t        if (keys %bats_count) {\n   726\t            print { $out_fh } \"<hr/><span class='bats-summary'>Summary:\";\n   727\t            my $total = 0;\n   728\t            my $comma = '';\n   729\t            for my $class (qw(passed failed skipped)) {\n   730\t                if (my $n = $bats_count{$class}) {\n   731\t                    printf { $out_fh } \"%s <span class='bats-%s'>%d %s</span>\",\n   732\t                        $comma, $class, $n, ucfirst($class);\n   733\t                    $total += $n;\n   734\t                    $comma = ',';\n   735\t                }\n   736\t            }\n   737\t\n   738\t            printf { $out_fh } \".
